# Graph Report - thingtime  (2026-10-27)

## Corpus Check
- 6418 files · ~15,498,883 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.
- Unclassified: 430 file(s) not represented in the graph (top: .log 297, (none) 41, .css 16)

## Summary
- 71125 nodes · 210011 edges · 1495 communities (1352 shown, 143 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 96% EXTRACTED · 4% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 8728 edges (avg confidence: 0.87)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `66f45680`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
